💡 Windows PowerShell 常用命令指南：
--------------------------------------------------
👉 只抓取不上传 (Crawl Only):
   $env:SYNC_CLASS_SCOPE="all"
   $env:SYNC_CLASS_GRADES="2025,2024,2023,2022"
   $env:SYNC_CLASS_MAX_CONCURRENCY="1"
   $env:SYNC_CLASS_REQUEST_DELAY_MS="900"
   $env:SYNC_CLASS_CRAWL_ONLY="true"
   npm run sync:class

👉 只上传本地缓存 (Upload Only):
   $env:SYNC_CLASS_CRAWL_ONLY=""
   $env:SYNC_CLASS_UPLOAD_ONLY="true"
   $env:SYNC_UPLOAD_CHUNK_SIZE="50"
   npm run sync:upload-cache

👉 强制重新上传本地缓存 (Force Restart Upload):
   $env:SYNC_UPLOAD_FORCE_RESTART="true"
   $env:SYNC_CLASS_UPLOAD_ONLY="true"
   $env:SYNC_UPLOAD_CHUNK_SIZE="50"
   npm run sync:class
--------------------------------------------------
